# Registry projection review

Reviewer: reviewer:claude-session:4df9e88c@2026-10-12
Author session: codex-session:1@2026-10-11
Receipt commit: 2269f2d2790f5bcef0a9ea4838cf27954619feda
Review mode: ordinary

Scope: 683 projected classification receipts (300 six-column, 383 seven-column). 683 ok, 0 rework, 0 hold. Identity projection only under A30; no truth re-review.

## Method

Lead ran its own verifier (source below), not the author verifier, from the worktree at 619d5484a:

`node --max-old-space-size=6000 verify.mjs <worktree> result.json`

For every receipt read from the receipt commit:
- the original receipt at `recoveryProvenance.receiptCommit:receiptPath` with `receiptClaimId` exists exactly once;
- after removing `claimId` (and `recoveryProvenance` from the projection), the two rows are deep-equal, including shownText, class, rationale and evidence;
- shownTextDigest is the SHA-256 of shownText;
- authoredBy is non-empty, not script-prefixed and not this reviewer;
- the original report at `reportCommit` has an ok Class-column row for `verdictClaimId` with the same class, unit digest and shown-text digest, with 7 columns exactly for history-snapshot and candidate-native-content;
- the current ID resolves in `working-identity-registry.json` to the same path, anchor and sourceUnitDigest.

Result: 683 rows, 683 distinct IDs, 683 pass, 0 fail; 8 original receipt artifacts, 9 original reports. Every request row (digests, evidence digest, note) was compared to the verifier output with 0 differences before its verdict was set to ok.

```js
// Independent Lead check: each projected receipt equals its original receipt except claimId,
// the original report has an ok Class-column row for it, and the current ID resolves in the registry.
import { execFileSync } from 'node:child_process';
import crypto from 'node:crypto';
import fs from 'node:fs';
import { isDeepStrictEqual } from 'node:util';
const root = process.argv[2], P = 'plans/260925-documentation-authority-unification';
const RC = '2269f2d2790f5bcef0a9ea4838cf27954619feda';
const show = (c, p) => execFileSync('git', ['show', `${c}:${p}`], { cwd: root, encoding: 'utf8', maxBuffer: 1 << 30 });
const sha = s => crypto.createHash('sha256').update(Buffer.from(s)).digest('hex');
const proj = JSON.parse(show(RC, `${P}/ledger/candidate-classifications-agent-coordination-recovery.json`));
const reg = JSON.parse(fs.readFileSync(`${root}/${P}/reports/phase-06/working-identity-registry.json`, 'utf8'));
const units = new Map(reg.units.map(u => [u.claimId, u]));
const cache = new Map(); const get = (c, p) => { const k = c + ':' + p; if (!cache.has(k)) cache.set(k, show(c, p)); return cache.get(k); };
const rowsOf = text => text.split(/\r?\n/).map(l => l.trim().split(/(?<!\\)\|/).slice(1, -1).map(c => c.trim().replace(/\\\|/g, '|'))).filter(c => c.length >= 6);
const out = [], fails = [], ids = new Set();
if (proj.version !== 1 || proj.receipts.length !== 683) fails.push('header');
for (const r of proj.receipts) {
  const why = [];
  if (ids.has(r.claimId)) why.push('dup-id'); ids.add(r.claimId);
  const pv = r.recoveryProvenance;
  const orig = JSON.parse(get(pv.receiptCommit, pv.receiptPath)).receipts.filter(x => x.claimId === pv.receiptClaimId);
  if (orig.length !== 1) why.push('orig-count-' + orig.length);
  else {
    const a = { ...orig[0] }; delete a.claimId; const b = { ...r }; delete b.claimId; delete b.recoveryProvenance;
    if (!isDeepStrictEqual(a, b)) why.push('fields-differ');
  }
  if (r.shownTextDigest !== sha(r.shownText)) why.push('shown-digest');
  if (!r.authoredBy || r.authoredBy.startsWith('script:') || /claude-session:4df9e88c/.test(r.authoredBy)) why.push('author');
  const ev = r.class === 'history-snapshot' || r.class === 'candidate-native-content';
  const row = rowsOf(get(pv.reportCommit, pv.report)).filter(c => c[0] === pv.verdictClaimId && c[1] === r.class && c[2] === 'ok' && c[3] === r.unitDigest && c[4] === r.shownTextDigest && c.length === (ev ? 7 : 6));
  if (row.length < 1) why.push('no-ok-row');
  const u = units.get(r.claimId);
  if (!u || u.sourcePath !== r.path || u.sourceAnchor !== r.anchor || u.sourceUnitDigest !== r.unitDigest) why.push('registry');
  const note = `identity projection of ${pv.report.split('/').pop()} row ${pv.verdictClaimId}; bytes equal`;
  if (why.length) fails.push(`${r.claimId}: ${why.join(',')}`);
  out.push({ claimId: r.claimId, cls: r.class, unitDigest: r.unitDigest, shown: r.shownTextDigest, evidence: ev ? row[0]?.[6] : null, ok: !why.length, pv, note });
}
fs.writeFileSync(process.argv[3], JSON.stringify(out));
console.log({ rows: proj.receipts.length, distinct: ids.size, ok: out.filter(o => o.ok).length, fails: fails.slice(0, 20), failCount: fails.length, artifacts: new Set(out.map(o => o.pv.receiptCommit + o.pv.receiptPath)).size, reports: new Set(out.map(o => o.pv.reportCommit + o.pv.report)).size });
```
